```rust
//! UNRUN constructor, wiring, normal/cap-refusal/error-transfer, and
//! security-negative witness tests for `b_scalar_completion`
//! (`b.helper.Scalar.exit` and `b.helper.Scalar.failure` on `RetRight / Add / Done`).
//!
//! These tests invoke the real `emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128` and
//! `emit_b_scalar_failure_add` constructors and evaluate/inspect the emitted
//! `midnight_zkir_v3::Instruction` stream directly. Symbolic/off-circuit
//! instruction evaluation here is distinct from real SDK `midnight_zk_stdlib`
//! Halo2 constraint compilation or proof generation, which is not run in this
//! source-authoring delivery.

use super::b_scalar_arithmetic::*;
use super::b_scalar_completion::*;
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn var(name: &str) -> Operand {
    Operand::Variable(Identifier(name.into()))
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct RawDataSlot {
    tag: u128,
    n: u128,
    bool_b: u128,
    fields_start: u128,
    fields_len: u128,
    rest: [u128; 2],
}

impl RawDataSlot {
    fn zero() -> Self {
        Self {
            tag: 0,
            n: 0,
            bool_b: 0,
            fields_start: 0,
            fields_len: 0,
            rest: [0, 0],
        }
    }

    fn u128_row(n: u128) -> Self {
        Self {
            tag: DATA_TAG_U128,
            n,
            bool_b: 0,
            fields_start: 0,
            fields_len: 0,
            rest: [0, 0],
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct ExitAddU128Fixture {
    case_id: &'static str,
    enable: u128,
    bounds_data_nodes: u128,
    pre_active_data: u128,
    post_active_data: u128,
    pre_data_rows: Vec<RawDataSlot>,
    post_data_rows: Vec<RawDataSlot>,
    pre_reg_a: u128,
    staged_u128: u128,
    applied: bool,
    pre_alloc_data_nodes: u128,
    post_alloc_data_nodes: u128,
    pre_remaining_work: u128,
    pre_consumed_work: u128,
    pre_helper_work: u128,
    pre_helper_visits: u128,
    post_remaining_work: u128,
    post_consumed_work: u128,
    post_helper_work: u128,
    post_helper_visits: u128,
}

impl ExitAddU128Fixture {
    fn new_applied(
        case_id: &'static str,
        capacity: usize,
        prefix_rows: Vec<RawDataSlot>,
        pre_alloc_data_nodes: u128,
        bounds_data_nodes: u128,
        pre_reg_a: u128,
        staged_u128: u128,
    ) -> Self {
        let pre_active = prefix_rows.len();
        assert!(pre_active < capacity);
        assert!(pre_alloc_data_nodes < bounds_data_nodes);
        let mut pre_data_rows = prefix_rows.clone();
        pre_data_rows.resize(capacity, RawDataSlot::zero());
        let mut post_data_rows = pre_data_rows.clone();
        post_data_rows[pre_active] = RawDataSlot::u128_row(staged_u128);
        Self {
            case_id,
            enable: 1,
            bounds_data_nodes,
            pre_active_data: pre_active as u128,
            post_active_data: (pre_active + 1) as u128,
            pre_data_rows,
            post_data_rows,
            pre_reg_a,
            staged_u128,
            applied: true,
            pre_alloc_data_nodes,
            post_alloc_data_nodes: pre_alloc_data_nodes + 1,
            pre_remaining_work: 82,
            pre_consumed_work: 18,
            pre_helper_work: 10,
            pre_helper_visits: 5,
            post_remaining_work: 81,
            post_consumed_work: 19,
            post_helper_work: 11,
            post_helper_visits: 5,
        }
    }

    fn new_cap_refused(
        case_id: &'static str,
        capacity: usize,
        prefix_rows: Vec<RawDataSlot>,
        pre_alloc_data_nodes: u128,
        bounds_data_nodes: u128,
        pre_reg_a: u128,
        staged_u128: u128,
    ) -> Self {
        let pre_active = prefix_rows.len();
        assert!(pre_active <= capacity);
        assert!(pre_alloc_data_nodes >= bounds_data_nodes);
        let mut pre_data_rows = prefix_rows;
        pre_data_rows.resize(capacity, RawDataSlot::zero());
        let post_data_rows = pre_data_rows.clone();
        Self {
            case_id,
            enable: 1,
            bounds_data_nodes,
            pre_active_data: pre_active as u128,
            post_active_data: pre_active as u128,
            pre_data_rows,
            post_data_rows,
            pre_reg_a,
            staged_u128,
            applied: false,
            pre_alloc_data_nodes,
            post_alloc_data_nodes: pre_alloc_data_nodes,
            pre_remaining_work: 82,
            pre_consumed_work: 18,
            pre_helper_work: 10,
            pre_helper_visits: 5,
            post_remaining_work: 81,
            post_consumed_work: 19,
            post_helper_work: 11,
            post_helper_visits: 5,
        }
    }

    fn build_symbolic_inputs(&self) -> (BScalarExitAddU128Inputs, BTreeMap<String, u128>) {
        let env = std::cell::RefCell::new(BTreeMap::new());
        let bind = |name: String, val: u128| -> Operand {
            env.borrow_mut().insert(name.clone(), val);
            Operand::Variable(Identifier(name))
        };
        let bind_u128 = |prefix: &str, val: u128| -> BU128Lanes {
            let bytes = val.to_le_bytes();
            std::array::from_fn(|i| bind(format!("{prefix}.{i}"), u128::from(bytes[i])))
        };
        let bind_data_row = |prefix: &str, slot: &RawDataSlot| -> BDataRowLanes {
            BDataRowLanes {
                tag: bind(format!("{prefix}.tag"), slot.tag),
                n: bind_u128(&format!("{prefix}.n"), slot.n),
                bool_b: bind(format!("{prefix}.bool"), slot.bool_b),
                fields_start: bind(format!("{prefix}.f_start"), slot.fields_start),
                fields_len: bind(format!("{prefix}.f_len"), slot.fields_len),
                rest: vec![
                    bind(format!("{prefix}.rest.0"), slot.rest[0]),
                    bind(format!("{prefix}.rest.1"), slot.rest[1]),
                ],
            }
        };

        let enable = bind("%in.enable".into(), self.enable);
        let bounds_data_nodes = bind("%in.bounds.data_nodes".into(), self.bounds_data_nodes);

        let pre_data = BDataTableLanes {
            capacity: self.pre_data_rows.len(),
            active_rows: bind("%in.pre.data.active".into(), self.pre_active_data),
            rows: self
                .pre_data_rows
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(r, s)| bind_data_row(&format!("%in.pre.data.row.{r}"), s))
                .collect(),
        };
        let post_data = BDataTableLanes {
            capacity: self.post_data_rows.len(),
            active_rows: bind("%in.post.data.active".into(), self.post_active_data),
            rows: self
                .post_data_rows
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(r, s)| bind_data_row(&format!("%in.post.data.row.{r}"), s))
                .collect(),
        };

        let pre_rest = BScalarHelperRestLanes {
            origin_reference_cursor: bind("%in.pre.h.rest.0".into(), 8),
            parent_resume_present: bind("%in.pre.h.rest.1".into(), 0),
            parent_resume_value: bind("%in.pre.h.rest.2".into(), 0),
            expected_present: bind("%in.pre.h.rest.3".into(), 0),
            expected_value: bind("%in.pre.h.rest.4".into(), 0),
        };
        let pre_helper = BScalarArithmeticRegister {
            present: bind("%in.pre.h.present".into(), HELPER_PRESENT),
            kind: bind("%in.pre.h.kind".into(), HELPER_KIND_SCALAR),
            site: bind("%in.pre.h.site".into(), SITE_RET_RIGHT),
            mode: bind("%in.pre.h.mode".into(), SCALAR_MODE_ADD),
            phase: bind("%in.pre.h.phase".into(), SCALAR_PHASE_DONE),
            operand_a: BValueRef {
                space: bind("%in.pre.h.op_a.space".into(), 0),
                index: bind("%in.pre.h.op_a.index".into(), 0),
            },
            operand_b: BOptValueRef {
                present: bind("%in.pre.h.op_b.present".into(), OPT_PRESENT),
                value: BValueRef {
                    space: bind("%in.pre.h.op_b.space".into(), 0),
                    index: bind("%in.pre.h.op_b.index".into(), 1),
                },
            },
            reg_a: bind_u128("%in.pre.h.reg_a", self.pre_reg_a),
            staged: BScalarStagedLanes {
                tag: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.tag".into(), SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_U128_VALUE),
                u128_value: bind_u128("%in.pre.h.staged.u128", self.staged_u128),
                bool_value: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.bool".into(), 0),
                pending_node_tag: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.pending".into(), 0),
                prim_error_code: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.err".into(), 0),
                selector: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.sel".into(), 0),
                type_test_ok: bind("%in.pre.h.staged.ok".into(), 0),
            },
            rest: pre_rest.to_rest_vec(),
        };

        let post_h_val = |v: u128| -> u128 { if self.applied { 0 } else { v } };
        let post_rest = BScalarHelperRestLanes {
            origin_reference_cursor: bind("%in.post.h.rest.0".into(), post_h_val(8)),
            parent_resume_present: bind("%in.post.h.rest.1".into(), 0),
            parent_resume_value: bind("%in.post.h.rest.2".into(), 0),
            expected_present: bind("%in.post.h.rest.3".into(), 0),
            expected_value: bind("%in.post.h.rest.4".into(), 0),
        };
        let post_helper = BScalarArithmeticRegister {
            present: bind("%in.post.h.present".into(), post_h_val(HELPER_PRESENT)),
            kind: bind("%in.post.h.kind".into(), post_h_val(HELPER_KIND_SCALAR)),
            site: bind("%in.post.h.site".into(), post_h_val(SITE_RET_RIGHT)),
            mode: bind("%in.post.h.mode".into(), post_h_val(SCALAR_MODE_ADD)),
            phase: bind("%in.post.h.phase".into(), post_h_val(SCALAR_PHASE_DONE)),
            operand_a: BValueRef {
                space: bind("%in.post.h.op_a.space".into(), 0),
                index: bind("%in.post.h.op_a.index".into(), 0),
            },
            operand_b: BOptValueRef {
                present: bind("%in.post.h.op_b.present".into(), post_h_val(OPT_PRESENT)),
                value: BValueRef {
                    space: bind("%in.post.h.op_b.space".into(), 0),
                    index: bind("%in.post.h.op_b.index".into(), post_h_val(1)),
                },
            },
            reg_a: bind_u128("%in.post.h.reg_a", post_h_val(self.pre_reg_a)),
            staged: BScalarStagedLanes {
                tag: bind(
                    "%in.post.h.staged.tag".into(),
                    post_h_val(SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_U128_VALUE),
                ),
                u128_value: bind_u128("%in.post.h.staged.u128", post_h_val(self.staged_u128)),
                bool_value: bind("%in.post.h.staged.bool".into(), 0),
                pending_node_tag: bind("%in.post.h.staged.pending".into(), 0),
                prim_error_code: bind("%in.post.h.staged.err".into(), 0),
                selector: bind("%in.post.h.staged.sel".into(), 0),
                type_test_ok: bind("%in.post.h.staged.ok".into(), 0),
            },
            rest: post_rest.to_rest_vec(),
        };

        let pre_result = BHelperResultLanes {
            present: bind("%in.pre.res.present".into(), 0),
            tag: bind("%in.pre.res.tag".into(), 0),
            scalar_value: BValueRef {
                space: bind("%in.pre.res.val.space".into(), 0),
                index: bind("%in.pre.res.val.index".into(), 0),
            },
            scalar_error_code: bind("%in.pre.res.err".into(), 0),
            scalar_selector: bind("%in.pre.res.sel".into(), 0),
            scalar_type_test_ok: bind("%in.pre.res.ok".into(), 0),
            rest: vec![bind("%in.pre.res.rest.0".into(), 0)],
        };
        let post_result = BHelperResultLanes {
            present: bind(
                "%in.post.res.present".into(),
                if self.applied { OPT_PRESENT } else { 0 },
            ),
            tag: bind(
                "%in.post.res.tag".into(),
                if self.applied {
                    HELPER_RESULT_TAG_SCALAR_VALUE
                } else {
                    0
                },
            ),
            scalar_value: BValueRef {
                space: bind(
                    "%in.post.res.val.space".into(),
                    if self.applied { VALUE_SPACE_DATA } else { 0 },
                ),
                index: bind(
                    "%in.post.res.val.index".into(),
                    if self.applied {
                        self.pre_active_data
                    } else {
                        0
                    },
                ),
            },
            scalar_error_code: bind("%in.post.res.err".into(), 0),
            scalar_selector: bind("%in.post.res.sel".into(), 0),
            scalar_type_test_ok: bind("%in.post.res.ok".into(), 0),
            rest: vec![bind("%in.post.res.rest.0".into(), 0)],
        };

        let pre_outcome = BOutcomeLanes {
            tag: bind("%in.pre.out.tag".into(), OUTCOME_TAG_RUNNING),
            halted_value: BValueRef {
                space: bind("%in.pre.out.halt.space".into(), 0),
                index: bind("%in.pre.out.halt.index".into(), 0),
            },
            rejected_code: bind("%in.pre.out.rej".into(), 0),
            exhausted_reason: bind("%in.pre.out.exh".into(), 0),
        };
        let post_outcome = BOutcomeLanes {
            tag: bind(
                "%in.post.out.tag".into(),
                if self.applied {
                    OUTCOME_TAG_RUNNING
                } else {
                    OUTCOME_TAG_EXHAUSTED
                },
            ),
            halted_value: BValueRef {
                space: bind("%in.post.out.halt.space".into(), 0),
                index: bind("%in.post.out.halt.index".into(), 0),
            },
            rejected_code: bind("%in.post.out.rej".into(), 0),
            exhausted_reason: bind(
                "%in.post.out.exh".into(),
                if self.applied {
                    0
                } else {
                    EXHAUST_REASON_DATA_NODES
                },
            ),
        };

        let pre_counters = BScalarCompletionCounters {
            work: BChargedCounters {
                remaining_work: bind_u128("%in.pre.cnt.rem", self.pre_remaining_work),
                consumed_work: bind_u128("%in.pre.cnt.con", self.pre_consumed_work),
                helper_work: bind_u128("%in.pre.cnt.hw", self.pre_helper_work),
                helper_visits: bind_u128("%in.pre.cnt.hv", self.pre_helper_visits),
            },
            reference_cursor: bind("%in.pre.cnt.ref_cur".into(), 8),
            alloc_data_nodes: bind("%in.pre.cnt.alloc_dn".into(), self.pre_alloc_data_nodes),
            alloc_data_fields: bind("%in.pre.cnt.alloc_df".into(), 2),
            alloc_environment_cells: bind("%in.pre.cnt.alloc_env".into(), 2),
            depth: bind("%in.pre.cnt.depth".into(), 1),
            task_live: bind("%in.pre.cnt.tasks".into(), 0),
            active_data_fields: bind("%in.pre.cnt.act_df".into(), 2),
            active_env: bind("%in.pre.cnt.act_env".into(), 2),
            active_value_slots: bind("%in.pre.cnt.act_vs".into(), 2),
            rest: vec![bind("%in.pre.cnt.rest.0".into(), 100)],
        };
        let post_counters = BScalarCompletionCounters {
            work: BChargedCounters {
                remaining_work: bind_u128("%in.post.cnt.rem", self.post_remaining_work),
                consumed_work: bind_u128("%in.post.cnt.con", self.post_consumed_work),
                helper_work: bind_u128("%in.post.cnt.hw", self.post_helper_work),
                helper_visits: bind_u128("%in.post.cnt.hv", self.post_helper_visits),
            },
            reference_cursor: bind("%in.post.cnt.ref_cur".into(), 8),
            alloc_data_nodes: bind("%in.post.cnt.alloc_dn".into(), self.post_alloc_data_nodes),
            alloc_data_fields: bind("%in.post.cnt.alloc_df".into(), 2),
            alloc_environment_cells: bind("%in.post.cnt.alloc_env".into(), 2),
            depth: bind("%in.post.cnt.depth".into(), 1),
            task_live: bind("%in.post.cnt.tasks".into(), 0),
            active_data_fields: bind("%in.post.cnt.act_df".into(), 2),
            active_env: bind("%in.post.cnt.act_env".into(), 2),
            active_value_slots: bind("%in.post.cnt.act_vs".into(), 2),
            rest: vec![bind("%in.post.cnt.rest.0".into(), 100)],
        };

        drop(bind_data_row);
        drop(bind_u128);
        drop(bind);
        (
            BScalarExitAddU128Inputs {
                enable,
                bounds_data_nodes,
                pre_data,
                post_data,
                pre_helper,
                post_helper,
                pre_result,
                post_result,
                pre_outcome,
                post_outcome,
                pre_counters,
                post_counters,
            },
            env.into_inner(),
        )
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct FailureAddFixture {
    case_id: &'static str,
    enable: u128,
    error_code: u128,
    pre_reg_a: u128,
    pre_data_rows: Vec<RawDataSlot>,
    active_data: u128,
    alloc_data_nodes: u128,
}

impl FailureAddFixture {
    fn new(case_id: &'static str, error_code: u128, pre_reg_a: u128) -> Self {
        Self {
            case_id,
            enable: 1,
            error_code,
            pre_reg_a,
            pre_data_rows: vec![RawDataSlot::u128_row(42), RawDataSlot::zero()],
            active_data: 1,
            alloc_data_nodes: 3,
        }
    }

    fn build_symbolic_inputs(&self) -> (BScalarFailureAddInputs, BTreeMap<String, u128>) {
        let exit_template = ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied(
            self.case_id,
            self.pre_data_rows.len(),
            vec![self.pre_data_rows[0].clone()],
            self.alloc_data_nodes,
            self.alloc_data_nodes + 1,
            self.pre_reg_a,
            0,
        );
        let (exit_inputs, mut env) = exit_template.build_symbolic_inputs();
        env.insert("%in.enable".into(), self.enable);
        env.insert("%in.pre.h.staged.tag".into(), SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_PRIM_ERROR);
        env.insert("%in.pre.h.staged.err".into(), self.error_code);
        env.insert("%in.post.data.active".into(), self.active_data);
        env.insert("%in.post.cnt.alloc_dn".into(), self.alloc_data_nodes);
        env.insert("%in.post.res.present".into(), OPT_PRESENT);
        env.insert("%in.post.res.tag".into(), HELPER_RESULT_TAG_SCALAR_ERROR);
        env.insert("%in.post.res.val.space".into(), 0);
        env.insert("%in.post.res.val.index".into(), 0);
        env.insert("%in.post.res.err".into(), self.error_code);
        env.insert("%in.post.out.tag".into(), OUTCOME_TAG_RUNNING);
        env.insert("%in.post.out.exh".into(), 0);

        (
            BScalarFailureAddInputs {
                enable: exit_inputs.enable,
                pre_data: exit_inputs.pre_data,
                post_data: exit_inputs.post_data,
                pre_helper: exit_inputs.pre_helper,
                post_helper: exit_inputs.post_helper,
                pre_result: exit_inputs.pre_result,
                post_result: exit_inputs.post_result,
                pre_outcome: exit_inputs.pre_outcome,
                post_outcome: exit_inputs.post_outcome,
                pre_counters: exit_inputs.pre_counters,
                post_counters: exit_inputs.post_counters,
            },
            env,
        )
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum EvalRejection {
    UnboundVariable(String),
    NonBoolean { index: usize, value: u128 },
    BitWidthExceeded { index: usize, value: u128, bits: u32 },
    ConstraintEqFailed { index: usize, left: u128, right: u128 },
    AssertFailed { index: usize, cond: u128 },
    UnsupportedInstruction { index: usize },
}

fn eval_operand(op: &Operand, env: &BTreeMap<String, u128>) -> Result<u128, EvalRejection> {
    match op {
        Operand::Variable(id) => env
            .get(&id.0)
            .copied()
            .ok_or_else(|| EvalRejection::UnboundVariable(id.0.clone())),
        Operand::Immediate(fr) => {
            for candidate in 0u128..=1024 {
                if *fr == Fr::from(candidate) {
                    return Ok(candidate);
                }
            }
            Err(EvalRejection::UnboundVariable(format!("immediate:{fr:?}")))
        }
    }
}

fn eval_emitted_instructions(
    instructions: &[Instruction],
    initial_env: &BTreeMap<String, u128>,
) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, u128>, EvalRejection> {
    let mut env = initial_env.clone();
    for (index, inst) in instructions.iter().enumerate() {
        match inst {
            Instruction::CondSelect { bit, a, b, output } => {
                let cond = eval_operand(bit, &env)?;
                if cond > 1 {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::NonBoolean { index, value: cond });
                }
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                env.insert(output.0.clone(), if cond == 1 { va } else { vb });
            }
            Instruction::ConstrainToBoolean { val } => {
                let v = eval_operand(val, &env)?;
                if v > 1 {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::NonBoolean { index, value: v });
                }
            }
            Instruction::ConstrainBits { val, bits } => {
                let v = eval_operand(val, &env)?;
                if *bits < 128 && (v >> *bits) != 0 {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::BitWidthExceeded {
                        index,
                        value: v,
                        bits: *bits,
                    });
                }
            }
            Instruction::ConstrainEq { a, b } => {
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                if va != vb {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::ConstraintEqFailed {
                        index,
                        left: va,
                        right: vb,
                    });
                }
            }
            Instruction::Assert { cond } => {
                let c = eval_operand(cond, &env)?;
                if c != 1 {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::AssertFailed { index, cond: c });
                }
            }
            Instruction::TestEq { a, b, output } => {
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                env.insert(output.0.clone(), u128::from(va == vb));
            }
            Instruction::LessThan { a, b, bits, output } => {
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                if *bits < 128 && ((va >> *bits) != 0 || (vb >> *bits) != 0) {
                    return Err(EvalRejection::BitWidthExceeded {
                        index,
                        value: va.max(vb),
                        bits: *bits,
                    });
                }
                env.insert(output.0.clone(), u128::from(va < vb));
            }
            Instruction::Add { a, b, output } => {
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                env.insert(output.0.clone(), va.checked_add(vb).unwrap());
            }
            Instruction::Mul { a, b, output } => {
                let va = eval_operand(a, &env)?;
                let vb = eval_operand(b, &env)?;
                env.insert(output.0.clone(), va.checked_mul(vb).unwrap());
            }
            _ => return Err(EvalRejection::UnsupportedInstruction { index }),
        }
    }
    Ok(env)
}

#[test]
fn exit_applied_covers_zero_nonzero_max_payloads_first_and_last_slots_and_prefix() {
    let existing_row_0 = RawDataSlot {
        tag: VALUE_TAG_BOOL,
        n: 0,
        bool_b: 1,
        fields_start: 0,
        fields_len: 0,
        rest: [7, 9],
    };
    let existing_row_1 = RawDataSlot::u128_row(65_536);

    let cases = vec![
        // First physical slot (index 0 of 2), zero payload, zero existing alloc_data_nodes
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied("first-slot-zero", 2, vec![], 0, 2, 0, 0),
        // Middle slot (index 1 of 3), small sum 16, non-zero alloc_data_nodes > active.data
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied(
            "mid-slot-nonzero-alloc",
            3,
            vec![existing_row_0.clone()],
            4,
            6,
            7,
            16,
        ),
        // Last physical slot (index 2 of 3), u128::MAX payload, exact cap boundary alloc+1 == bounds
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied(
            "last-slot-max-at-cap-boundary",
            3,
            vec![existing_row_0, existing_row_1],
            9,
            10,
            u128::MAX - 1,
            u128::MAX,
        ),
    ];

    for case in cases {
        let (inputs, env) = case.build_symbolic_inputs();
        let block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&inputs, "exit_ok").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(block.preserved, inputs, "case {}", case.case_id);
        let evaluated = eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &env)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("case {} rejected: {e:?}", case.case_id));

        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.applied, &evaluated).unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.cap_refused, &evaluated).unwrap(), 0);
        assert_eq!(
            eval_operand(&block.allocated_index, &evaluated).unwrap(),
            case.pre_active_data
        );
        let mut alloc_bytes = [0u8; 16];
        for i in 0..16 {
            alloc_bytes[i] = eval_operand(&block.allocated_n[i], &evaluated).unwrap() as u8;
        }
        assert_eq!(u128::from_le_bytes(alloc_bytes), case.staged_u128);
        for (slot, sel) in block.write_selectors.iter().enumerate() {
            assert_eq!(
                eval_operand(sel, &evaluated).unwrap(),
                u128::from(slot as u128 == case.pre_active_data)
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn exit_refused_cap_charged_retains_helper_and_arenas_and_sets_exhausted_data_nodes() {
    let cases = vec![
        // Zero capacity / zero bound: first allocation triggers Exhausted(DataNodes), charged
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_cap_refused("zero-bound-cap-refused", 0, vec![], 0, 0, 7, 16),
        // Logical alloc_data_nodes == bounds_data_nodes even when physical slot exists
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_cap_refused(
            "logical-cap-reached-with-free-physical-slot",
            3,
            vec![RawDataSlot::u128_row(99)],
            5,
            5,
            100,
            250,
        ),
    ];

    for case in cases {
        let (inputs, env) = case.build_symbolic_inputs();
        let block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&inputs, "exit_cap").unwrap();
        let evaluated = eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &env)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("case {} rejected: {e:?}", case.case_id));
        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.applied, &evaluated).unwrap(), 0);
        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.cap_refused, &evaluated).unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.allocated_index, &evaluated).unwrap(), 0);
        for i in 0..16 {
            assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.allocated_n[i], &evaluated).unwrap(), 0);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn failure_transfers_codes_10_and_11_clears_helper_and_preserves_data_and_visits() {
    for (case_id, code) in [
        ("overflow-10", PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW),
        ("sort-mismatch-11", PRIM_ERROR_SORT_MISMATCH),
    ] {
        let fixture = FailureAddFixture::new(case_id, code, 42);
        let (inputs, env) = fixture.build_symbolic_inputs();
        let block = emit_b_scalar_failure_add(&inputs, "fail_ok").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(block.preserved, inputs);
        let evaluated = eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &env)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("case {case_id} rejected: {e:?}"));

        assert_eq!(eval_operand(&block.error_code, &evaluated).unwrap(), code);
        assert_eq!(
            eval_operand(&block.is_overflow_10, &evaluated).unwrap(),
            u128::from(code == PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            eval_operand(&block.is_sort_mismatch_11, &evaluated).unwrap(),
            u128::from(code == PRIM_ERROR_SORT_MISMATCH)
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn security_negative_exit_applied_mutations_are_rejected_in_circuit() {
    let fixture = ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied(
        "neg-applied",
        2,
        vec![RawDataSlot::u128_row(55)],
        3,
        5,
        7,
        16,
    );
    let (inputs, base_env) = fixture.build_symbolic_inputs();
    let block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&inputs, "exit_neg").unwrap();

    let assert_rejects = |key: &str, bad: u128, label: &str| {
        let mut mutated = base_env.clone();
        mutated.insert(key.into(), bad);
        assert!(
            eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &mutated).is_err(),
            "expected circuit rejection for {label} ({key} = {bad})"
        );
    };

    // 1. Pre-state guards (phase, mode, site, staged tag, outcome, absent result)
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.h.phase", SCALAR_PHASE_SECOND, "pre.phase != Done");
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.pre.h.staged.tag",
        SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_PRIM_ERROR,
        "pre.staged.tag == PrimError on exit",
    );
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.out.tag", OUTCOME_TAG_EXHAUSTED, "pre.outcome != Running");
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.res.present", 1, "pre.result already present");

    // 2. Appended row payload, tag, inactive union columns, and existing prefix row preservation
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.row.1.tag", VALUE_TAG_BOOL, "new DataRow wrong tag");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.row.1.n.0", 17, "new DataRow wrong U128 byte 0");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.row.1.bool", 1, "new DataRow dirty inactive bool_b");
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.post.data.row.1.f_start",
        1,
        "new DataRow dirty inactive fields_start",
    );
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.row.1.rest.0", 1, "new DataRow dirty rest column");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.row.0.n.0", 54, "existing prefix row 0 mutated");
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.data.row.1.n.0", 1, "inactive pre slot 1 non-zero");

    // 3. New DataRef / result / active.data / alloc.dataNodes deltas
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.active", 1, "active.data not incremented");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.alloc_dn", 3, "alloc.dataNodes not incremented");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.res.present", 0, "post.result not set");
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.post.res.tag",
        HELPER_RESULT_TAG_SCALAR_ERROR,
        "post.result wrong tag",
    );
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.post.res.val.space",
        VALUE_SPACE_CONST,
        "post.result space != Data",
    );
    assert_rejects("%in.post.res.val.index", 0, "post.result index != pre.active.data");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.res.err", 10, "post.result dirty error_code");

    // 4. clearHelper obligations (every post.helper column must be zeroed)
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.present", 1, "post.helper.present not cleared");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.site", SITE_RET_RIGHT, "post.helper.site not cleared");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.reg_a.0", 7, "post.helper.reg_a not cleared");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.staged.u128.0", 16, "post.helper.staged.u128 not cleared");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.rest.0", 8, "post.helper.rest[0] not cleared");

    // 5. Counter preservation: helperVisits must NOT increment at exit!
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.hv.0", 6, "helperVisits incremented at exit");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.rem.0", 82, "remainingWork not decremented");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.con.0", 18, "consumedWork not incremented");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.hw.0", 10, "helperWork not incremented");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.ref_cur", 9, "referenceCursor mutated at exit");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.depth", 2, "depth mutated at exit");
}

#[test]
fn security_negative_cap_branch_and_physical_capacity_mismatch_are_rejected() {
    // 1. When alloc_data_nodes == bounds_data_nodes, attempting to apply allocation/clearHelper fails.
    let mut forged_apply = ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied(
        "forged-apply-at-cap",
        2,
        vec![RawDataSlot::u128_row(1)],
        5,
        6,
        7,
        16,
    );
    forged_apply.bounds_data_nodes = 5; // now pre_alloc (5) >= bounds (5), so cap_refused == 1!
    let (inputs, env) = forged_apply.build_symbolic_inputs();
    let block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&inputs, "exit_forged_apply").unwrap();
    assert!(eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &env).is_err());

    // 2. On RefusedCapCharged, clearing helper or mutating outcome reason must fail.
    let cap_case = ExitAddU128Fixture::new_cap_refused(
        "cap-refused-strict",
        2,
        vec![RawDataSlot::u128_row(1)],
        5,
        5,
        7,
        16,
    );
    let (cap_inputs, cap_env) = cap_case.build_symbolic_inputs();
    let cap_block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&cap_inputs, "exit_cap_strict").unwrap();

    let mut cleared_on_cap = cap_env.clone();
    cleared_on_cap.insert("%in.post.h.present".into(), 0);
    assert!(eval_emitted_instructions(&cap_block.instructions, &cleared_on_cap).is_err());

    let mut wrong_reason = cap_env.clone();
    wrong_reason.insert("%in.post.out.exh".into(), EXHAUST_REASON_DATA_FIELDS);
    assert!(eval_emitted_instructions(&cap_block.instructions, &wrong_reason).is_err());

    // 3. E04 physical capacity bound isolation: when logical cap allows allocation
    // (pre.alloc.dataNodes = 5 < bounds.dataNodes = 10, so applied == 1) and E03
    // logical counter increment is satisfied (post.alloc.dataNodes = 6), a witness
    // at physical capacity (pre.active.data = 2 == capacity) with consistent Applied
    // delta (post.active.data = 3) reaches E04 and is rejected at the E04
    // `post_exceeds_cap` check (`b.eq(post_exceeds_cap, immediate(0))` at index 144,
    // which precedes `b.assert(guarded_physical_cap)` at index 148 and
    // `b.gated_eq(enable, eff_post_active_data, active_data_plus)` at index 152).
    let mut phys_full = cap_env;
    phys_full.insert("%in.bounds.data_nodes".into(), 10);
    phys_full.insert("%in.post.cnt.alloc_dn".into(), 6);
    phys_full.insert("%in.pre.data.active".into(), 2); // equals capacity 2
    phys_full.insert("%in.post.data.active".into(), 3);
    assert_eq!(
        cap_block.instructions[142],
        Instruction::LessThan {
            a: immediate(2),
            b: var("%exit_cap_strict.local.temp.68"),
            bits: 128,
            output: Identifier("%exit_cap_strict.local.temp.70".into()),
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        cap_block.instructions[144],
        Instruction::ConstrainEq {
            a: var("%exit_cap_strict.local.temp.70"),
            b: immediate(0),
        }
    );
    assert_eq!(
        eval_emitted_instructions(&cap_block.instructions, &phys_full),
        Err(EvalRejection::ConstraintEqFailed {
            index: 144,
            left: 1,
            right: 0,
        })
    );
}

#[test]
fn security_negative_failure_mutations_are_rejected_in_circuit() {
    let fixture = FailureAddFixture::new("neg-fail", PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW, 7);
    let (inputs, base_env) = fixture.build_symbolic_inputs();
    let block = emit_b_scalar_failure_add(&inputs, "fail_neg").unwrap();

    let assert_rejects = |key: &str, bad: u128, label: &str| {
        let mut mutated = base_env.clone();
        mutated.insert(key.into(), bad);
        assert!(
            eval_emitted_instructions(&block.instructions, &mutated).is_err(),
            "expected circuit rejection for {label} ({key} = {bad})"
        );
    };

    assert_rejects("%in.pre.h.staged.err", 9, "invalid ErrorCode 9 not in {10,11}");
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.h.staged.err", 12, "invalid ErrorCode 12 not in {10,11}");
    assert_rejects("%in.pre.h.staged.u128.0", 1, "non-zero inactive staged u128 byte");
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.post.res.err",
        PRIM_ERROR_SORT_MISMATCH,
        "mismatched result error code 11 vs 10",
    );
    assert_rejects(
        "%in.post.out.tag",
        OUTCOME_TAG_REJECTED,
        "premature Rejected outcome publication at HelperFail",
    );
    assert_rejects("%in.post.data.active", 2, "spurious Data allocation on failure");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.alloc_dn", 4, "spurious alloc.dataNodes increment");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.h.present", 1, "helper not cleared on failure");
    assert_rejects("%in.post.cnt.hv.0", 6, "helperVisits incremented on failure");
}

#[test]
fn disabled_exit_and_failure_branches_do_not_constrain_post_state() {
    let mut exit_disabled =
        ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied("disabled-exit", 1, vec![], 0, 1, 7, 16);
    exit_disabled.enable = 0;
    exit_disabled.post_active_data = 99;
    exit_disabled.post_alloc_data_nodes = 99;
    exit_disabled.post_remaining_work = 999;
    let (exit_inputs, mut exit_env) = exit_disabled.build_symbolic_inputs();
    exit_env.insert("%in.pre.h.phase".into(), SCALAR_PHASE_FIRST);
    exit_env.insert("%in.post.out.tag".into(), OUTCOME_TAG_REJECTED);
    let exit_block = emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&exit_inputs, "exit_dis").unwrap();
    let eval_exit = eval_emitted_instructions(&exit_block.instructions, &exit_env).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(eval_operand(&exit_block.applied, &eval_exit).unwrap(), 0);
    assert_eq!(eval_operand(&exit_block.cap_refused, &eval_exit).unwrap(), 0);

    let mut fail_disabled = FailureAddFixture::new("disabled-fail", PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW, 7);
    fail_disabled.enable = 0;
    let (fail_inputs, mut fail_env) = fail_disabled.build_symbolic_inputs();
    fail_env.insert("%in.pre.h.staged.err".into(), 0);
    fail_env.insert("%in.post.res.present".into(), 0);
    let fail_block = emit_b_scalar_failure_add(&fail_inputs, "fail_dis").unwrap();
    let eval_fail = eval_emitted_instructions(&fail_block.instructions, &fail_env).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(eval_operand(&fail_block.error_code, &eval_fail).unwrap(), 0);
}

#[test]
fn completion_constructors_reject_invalid_scopes_shapes_and_temp_collisions() {
    let fixture = ExitAddU128Fixture::new_applied("ctor", 1, vec![], 0, 1, 0, 0);
    let (base, _) = fixture.build_symbolic_inputs();

    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&base, ""),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::Fetch(
            ConstructionError::EmptyScope
        ))
    );

    let mut bad_cap = base.clone();
    bad_cap.pre_data.capacity = 2;
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&bad_cap, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::DataRowCount)
    );

    let mut bad_row_rest = base.clone();
    bad_row_rest.post_data.rows[0].rest.pop();
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&bad_row_rest, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::DataRowRestLaneCount)
    );

    let mut bad_h_rest = base.clone();
    bad_h_rest.post_helper.rest.pop();
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&bad_h_rest, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::HelperRestLaneCount)
    );

    let mut bad_res_rest = base.clone();
    bad_res_rest.post_result.rest.pop();
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&bad_res_rest, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::ResultRestLaneCount)
    );

    let mut bad_cnt_rest = base.clone();
    bad_cnt_rest.post_counters.rest.pop();
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&bad_cnt_rest, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::CounterRestLaneCount)
    );

    let mut unprefixed = base.clone();
    unprefixed.bounds_data_nodes = var("bad_var");
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&unprefixed, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::Fetch(
            ConstructionError::UnprefixedVariable
        ))
    );

    let mut colliding = base;
    colliding.post_outcome.exhausted_reason = var("%s.local.temp.0");
    assert_eq!(
        emit_b_scalar_exit_add_u128(&colliding, "s"),
        Err(BScalarCompletionConstructionError::Fetch(
            ConstructionError::TemporaryNameCollision
        ))
    );
}
```

## W3-10 FULL FILE crates/mori-backend/src/sherlock_machine/b_scalar_subtraction.rs

```rust
//! Handwritten B-core `midnight_zkir_v3` instruction emitter for
//! `b.helper.Scalar.arithmetic` on branch `site = RetRight`, `mode = Sub`,
//! `phase = Second -> Done` (`/helper_events/2` in `rows-r4.json`).
//!
//! Checked `UInt128` subtraction `pre.reg_a - u128(operandB)` (left minus right).
//! The pinned SDK has no subtraction instruction; the 16-byte little-endian
//! borrow chain uses `Add`, `Mul`, `LessThan` and `ConstrainEq` over 8/9-bit
//! ranged lanes, so no relation wraps in the field. An in-range difference
//! stages `U128Value`, a final borrow stages `PrimError(10)`, and a valid
//! non-`UInt` operandB stages `PrimError(11)`.
//!
//! Carrier types and constants below are module-local structural copies of
//! `b_scalar_arithmetic` (identical lane order and packing), not the same Rust
//! types. The Add-specialized completion, return-right and P1 publication
//! emitters do not consume this block.
//!
//! Disabled rows (`enable = 0`) still require boolean `enable`, well-formed
//! nonzero-capacity tables (enabled-fetch cell ranges and
//! `active_rows <= capacity`) and zero enable-effective temporaries. A
//! zero-capacity table emits no fetch block: its read only forces that fetch's
//! enable to 0, so its `active_rows` is not bounded by capacity here. Every
//! supplied post lane is otherwise
//! unconstrained, so disabled-row stutter is not established here and remains
//! the dispatch owner's obligation.
//!
//! Source only: emits pinned SDK instructions over caller-supplied operands;
//! performs no SDK load, check, evaluation, compilation, or proof.
//!
//! Local carriers are logical lanes (16 little-endian byte lanes for `UInt128`
//! payloads and counters, schema enum ordinals for discriminators, and explicit
//! union payload lanes for `ScalarStaged`), not a frozen physical B width table
//! or serialized profile. `AB`, `P0`..`P3`, `PS`, exactly-one dispatch, full
//! `StepInv` (`SI-01`..`SI-23`, including `SI-22 S2` on `pre.staged`),
//! untouched-image complement preservation (`C`), and statement/field binding
//! (`L04`) remain explicit caller obligations.

use super::*;

pub const HELPER_PRESENT: u128 = 1; // notation Opt<A>.present
pub const OPT_ABSENT: u128 = 0; // notation Opt<A>.present = 0
pub const OPT_PRESENT: u128 = 1; // notation Opt<A>.present = 1
pub const HELPER_KIND_SCALAR: u128 = 0; // enums.HelperKind[0] = "Scalar"
pub const SITE_RET_RIGHT: u128 = 11; // enums.SiteTag[11] = "RetRight"
pub const SCALAR_MODE_SUB: u128 = 1; // enums.ScalarMode[1] = "Sub"
pub const SCALAR_PHASE_FIRST: u128 = 0; // enums.ScalarPhase[0] = "First"
pub const SCALAR_PHASE_SECOND: u128 = 1; // enums.ScalarPhase[1] = "Second"
pub const SCALAR_PHASE_DONE: u128 = 2; // enums.ScalarPhase[2] = "Done"

pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_NONE: u128 = 0; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[0]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_U128_VALUE: u128 = 1; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[1]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_BOOL_VALUE: u128 = 2; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[2]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_PENDING_NODE: u128 = 3; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[3]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_PRIM_ERROR: u128 = 4; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[4]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_SELECTOR: u128 = 5; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[5]
pub const SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_TYPE_TEST: u128 = 6; // enums.ScalarStagedTag[6]

pub const PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW: u128 = 10; // ErrorCode 10: checked overflow/underflow
pub const PRIM_ERROR_SORT_MISMATCH: u128 = 11; // ErrorCode 11: sort mismatch

pub const CONST_TAG_U128: u128 = 0; // enums.ConstTag[0] = "U128"
pub const DATA_TAG_U128: u128 = 0; // enums.DataTag[0] = "U128"
pub const VALUE_TAG_U128: u128 = 0; // LogicalKind::UInt ordinal 0
pub const VALUE_TAG_BOOL: u128 = 1; // LogicalKind::Bool ordinal 1
pub const VALUE_TAG_NIL: u128 = 2; // LogicalKind::Nil ordinal 2
pub const VALUE_TAG_CONS: u128 = 3; // LogicalKind::Cons ordinal 3
pub const VALUE_TAG_LEAF: u128 = 4; // LogicalKind::Leaf ordinal 4
pub const VALUE_TAG_BRANCH: u128 = 5; // LogicalKind::Branch ordinal 5
pub const VALID_VALUE_TAG_COUNT: u128 = 6; // core value kinds {UInt,Bool,Nil,Cons,Leaf,Branch}

/// UInt128 as 16 little-endian byte lanes; each enabled lane lies in [0, 255].
pub type BU128Lanes = [Operand; 16];

/// Encode a host `u128` into 16 little-endian immediate byte operands.
pub fn u128_to_le_immediates(n: u128) -> BU128Lanes {
    let bytes = n.to_le_bytes();
    std::array::from_fn(|i| immediate(u128::from(bytes[i])))
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BValueSpace {
    Const,
    Data,
}

/// `records.ValueRef`: space bit (`0` = Const, `1` = Data) and index lane.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BValueRef {
    pub space: Operand,
    pub index: Operand,
}

/// `Opt<ValueRef>`: presence bit (`0` = absent, `1` = present) and `ValueRef`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BOptValueRef {
    pub present: Operand,
    pub value: BValueRef,
}

/// Projection of one value-table row: variant tag ordinal lane and `U128.n` byte lanes.
/// By `/notation/inactive`, non-`U128` rows have all 16 `U128.n` lanes equal to zero.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BValueRowLanes {
    pub tag: Operand,
    pub n: BU128Lanes,
}

/// Every physical row of one value space, active or not; `capacity` may be 0.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BValueTableRead {
    pub capacity: usize,
    pub active_rows: Operand,
    pub rows: Vec<BValueRowLanes>,
}

/// `records.HelperControl.ScalarStaged`: discriminator `tag` plus union payload
/// columns. By `/notation/inactive`, every inactive union column must be zero.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BScalarStagedLanes {
    pub tag: Operand,
    pub u128_value: BU128Lanes,
    pub bool_value: Operand,
    pub pending_node_tag: Operand,
    pub prim_error_code: Operand,
    pub selector: Operand,
    pub type_test_ok: Operand,
}

/// Declarative carrier for the remaining `HelperControl::Scalar` fields from
/// `SAFE-INPUTS.json` (`originReferenceCursor`, `parentResume.{present,value}`,
/// `expected.{present,value}`) when mapped into `BScalarArithmeticRegister::rest`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BScalarHelperRestLanes {
    pub origin_reference_cursor: Operand,
    pub parent_resume_present: Operand,
    pub parent_resume_value: Operand,
    pub expected_present: Operand,
    pub expected_value: Operand,
}

impl BScalarHelperRestLanes {
    pub fn to_rest_vec(&self) -> Vec<Operand> {
        vec![
            self.origin_reference_cursor.clone(),
            self.parent_resume_present.clone(),
            self.parent_resume_value.clone(),
            self.expected_present.clone(),
            self.expected_value.clone(),
        ]
    }
}

/// `reg.helper` lanes in a `Scalar.arithmetic` view. Additive extension of the
/// archived `005` `BScalarRegister` carrier with explicit `operand_b` and `staged`
/// views. `rest` holds every remaining helper lane in identical pre and post order.
/// `pre.staged` is not constrained in-circuit (`SI-22 S2` is an external pre-StepInv
/// caller obligation); every `post.staged` lane is constrained when `enable = 1`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BScalarArithmeticRegister {
    pub present: Operand,
    pub kind: Operand,
    pub site: Operand,
    pub mode: Operand,
    pub phase: Operand,
    pub operand_a: BValueRef,
    pub operand_b: BOptValueRef,
    pub reg_a: BU128Lanes,
    pub staged: BScalarStagedLanes,
    pub rest: Vec<Operand>,
}

/// The four delta-vector entries changed by `T-HELPER` (`[-1, +1, +1, +1, 0..]`),
/// represented as local 16-byte little-endian `UInt128` carriers.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BChargedCounters {
    pub remaining_work: BU128Lanes,
    pub consumed_work: BU128Lanes,
    pub helper_work: BU128Lanes,
    pub helper_visits: BU128Lanes,
}

/// `post_*` are independent supplied witnesses; every enabled post lane named
/// here is constrained in-circuit. Value tables are read-only aliases.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BScalarArithmeticSubInputs {
    pub enable: Operand,
    pub constants: BValueTableRead,
    pub data: BValueTableRead,
    pub pre_helper: BScalarArithmeticRegister,
    pub post_helper: BScalarArithmeticRegister,
    pub pre_counters: BChargedCounters,
    pub post_counters: BChargedCounters,
}

/// Composable instruction fragment for `b.helper.Scalar.arithmetic` (`RetRight/Sub/Second`).
/// Contains no `PublicInput` or `Output` instructions. `read_tag`, `read_n`, and
/// `b_is_uint` are enable-effective (zero when `enable = 0`).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct BScalarArithmeticSubBlock {
    pub instructions: Vec<Instruction>,
    pub read_tag: Operand,
    pub read_n: BU128Lanes,
    pub b_is_uint: Operand,
    pub underflow: Operand,
    pub borrow_chain: [Operand; 16],
    pub preserved: BScalarArithmeticSubInputs,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BScalarArithmeticConstructionError {
    RowCount { space: BValueSpace },
    RestLaneCount,
    Fetch(ConstructionError),
}

impl From<ConstructionError> for BScalarArithmeticConstructionError {
    fn from(e: ConstructionError) -> Self {
        Self::Fetch(e)
    }
}

/// Emit the in-circuit relation for `b.helper.Scalar.arithmetic` on branch
/// `site = RetRight`, `mode = Sub`, `phase = Second -> Done`: checked
/// `pre.reg_a - u128(operandB)` (left minus right) staged per the module docs.
pub fn emit_b_scalar_arithmetic_sub(
    input: &BScalarArithmeticSubInputs,
    scope: &str,
) -> Result<BScalarArithmeticSubBlock, BScalarArithmeticConstructionError> {
    if scope.is_empty() {
        return Err(ConstructionError::EmptyScope.into());
    }
    if input.constants.rows.len() != input.constants.capacity {
        return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RowCount {
            space: BValueSpace::Const,
        });
    }
    if input.data.rows.len() != input.data.capacity {
        return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RowCount {
            space: BValueSpace::Data,
        });
    }
    if input.pre_helper.rest.len() != input.post_helper.rest.len() {
        return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RestLaneCount);
    }

    let namespaces = [
        format!("%{scope}.local.temp."),
        format!("%{scope}.const_fetch.temp."),
        format!("%{scope}.data_fetch.temp."),
    ];
    for operand in [
        &input.enable,
        &input.constants.active_rows,
        &input.data.active_rows,
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .chain(table_operands(&input.constants))
    .chain(table_operands(&input.data))
    .chain(helper_operands(&input.pre_helper))
    .chain(helper_operands(&input.post_helper))
    .chain(counter_operands(&input.pre_counters))
    .chain(counter_operands(&input.post_counters))
    {
        if let Operand::Variable(id) = operand {
            if !id.0.starts_with('%') {
                return Err(ConstructionError::UnprefixedVariable.into());
            }
            if namespaces.iter().any(|prefix| id.0.starts_with(prefix)) {
                return Err(ConstructionError::TemporaryNameCollision.into());
            }
        }
    }

    let mut b = Emitter {
        scope: scope.into(),
        serial: 0,
        code: Vec::new(),
    };
    let enable = &input.enable;

    // A01-A03: boolean enable and effective operandB space selection (0 Const, 1 Data).
    b.boolean(enable.clone());
    let space = b.select(
        enable.clone(),
        input.pre_helper.operand_b.value.space.clone(),
        immediate(0),
    );
    b.boolean(space.clone());
    let en_const = b.select(space.clone(), immediate(0), enable.clone());
    b.boolean(en_const.clone());

    // A04-A06: enabled value-table fetches for operandB across Const and Data spaces.
    let (tag_const, n_const) = space_read_any_kind(
        &mut b,
        &input.constants,
        en_const,
        input.pre_helper.operand_b.value.index.clone(),
        format!("{scope}.const_fetch"),
    )?;
    let (tag_data, n_data) = space_read_any_kind(
        &mut b,
        &input.data,
        space.clone(),
        input.pre_helper.operand_b.value.index.clone(),
        format!("{scope}.data_fetch"),
    )?;

    // A07: selected operandB tag must be a valid core value kind in 0..=5
    // ({UInt=0, Bool=1, Nil=2, Cons=3, Leaf=4, Branch=5}); reserved/Marker tags rejected.
    let read_tag = b.select(space.clone(), tag_data, tag_const);
    b.bits(read_tag.clone(), 3);
    let valid_tag = b.less_than(read_tag.clone(), immediate(VALID_VALUE_TAG_COUNT));
    b.assert(valid_tag);

    let tag_is_u128 = b.test_eq(read_tag.clone(), immediate(VALUE_TAG_U128));
    let b_is_uint = b.select(enable.clone(), tag_is_u128, immediate(0));
    b.boolean(b_is_uint.clone());

    // A08: selected operandB U128.n bytes; ranged to 8 bits and forced to 0 on non-UInt rows.
    let read_n = std::array::from_fn(|i| {
        let value = b.select(space.clone(), n_data[i].clone(), n_const[i].clone());
        b.bits(value.clone(), 8);
        let active_n = b.select(b_is_uint.clone(), value.clone(), immediate(0));
        b.eq(value.clone(), active_n);
        value
    });

    // A09: pre-state branch guards for RetRight / Sub / Second with operandB present.
    for (pre, expected) in [
        (&input.pre_helper.present, HELPER_PRESENT),
        (&input.pre_helper.kind, HELPER_KIND_SCALAR),
        (&input.pre_helper.site, SITE_RET_RIGHT),
        (&input.pre_helper.mode, SCALAR_MODE_SUB),
        (&input.pre_helper.phase, SCALAR_PHASE_SECOND),
        (&input.pre_helper.operand_b.present, OPT_PRESENT),
    ] {
        b.gated_eq(enable, pre.clone(), immediate(expected));
    }

    // A10: enable-effective 8-bit range on pre.reg_a and post.staged.u128_value.
    let eff_reg_a = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.pre_helper.reg_a);
    let eff_post_u128 = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.post_helper.staged.u128_value);

    // A11: 16-byte little-endian subtraction borrow chain for eff_reg_a - read_n
    // (left minus right). need[i] = read_n[i] + borrow_in lies in [0, 256] and
    // borrow_out = (eff_reg_a[i] < need[i]) is a constrained comparison bit.
    let mut borrow = immediate(0);
    let mut needs: [Operand; 16] = std::array::from_fn(|_| immediate(0));
    let mut borrow_chain: [Operand; 16] = std::array::from_fn(|_| immediate(0));
    for i in 0..16 {
        let need = b.add(read_n[i].clone(), borrow);
        b.bits(need.clone(), 9);
        let next_borrow = b.less_than(eff_reg_a[i].clone(), need.clone());
        needs[i] = need;
        borrow_chain[i] = next_borrow.clone();
        borrow = next_borrow;
    }
    let underflow = borrow;

    // A12: outcome selectors under enable:
    // - is_val = 1 iff enable = 1, b_is_uint = 1, and underflow = 0 (stages U128Value)
    // - otherwise when enable = 1: stages PrimError(10) if b_is_uint = 1 else PrimError(11)
    let not_underflow = b.select(underflow.clone(), immediate(0), immediate(1));
    b.boolean(not_underflow.clone());
    let is_val = b.select(b_is_uint.clone(), not_underflow, immediate(0));
    b.boolean(is_val.clone());

    // Per byte, over 9-bit integers (no field wrap):
    // post[i] + need[i] = eff_reg_a[i] + 256 * borrow_out[i]. With borrow_out fixed
    // by LessThan and post[i] ranged to 8 bits, post[i] is the unique byte difference.
    for i in 0..16 {
        let borrow_term = b.mul(immediate(256), borrow_chain[i].clone());
        b.bits(borrow_term.clone(), 9);
        let minuend = b.add(eff_reg_a[i].clone(), borrow_term);
        b.bits(minuend.clone(), 9);
        let reconstructed = b.add(eff_post_u128[i].clone(), needs[i].clone());
        b.bits(reconstructed.clone(), 9);
        b.gated_eq(&is_val, reconstructed, minuend);
        // Inactive U128Value payload columns must be 0 when PrimError is staged (or disabled).
        let inactive_u128 = b.select(is_val.clone(), immediate(0), eff_post_u128[i].clone());
        b.eq(inactive_u128, immediate(0));
    }

    // A13: post.phase = Done and post.staged discriminator + error code + inactive columns.
    b.gated_eq(
        enable,
        input.post_helper.phase.clone(),
        immediate(SCALAR_PHASE_DONE),
    );
    let expected_staged_tag = b.select(
        is_val,
        immediate(SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_U128_VALUE),
        immediate(SCALAR_STAGED_TAG_PRIM_ERROR),
    );
    b.bits(expected_staged_tag.clone(), 3);
    b.gated_eq(
        enable,
        input.post_helper.staged.tag.clone(),
        expected_staged_tag,
    );

    let code_if_uint = b.select(
        underflow.clone(),
        immediate(PRIM_ERROR_ARITH_OVERFLOW),
        immediate(0),
    );
    b.bits(code_if_uint.clone(), 4);
    let expected_error_code = b.select(
        b_is_uint.clone(),
        code_if_uint,
        immediate(PRIM_ERROR_SORT_MISMATCH),
    );
    b.bits(expected_error_code.clone(), 4);
    b.gated_eq(
        enable,
        input.post_helper.staged.prim_error_code.clone(),
        expected_error_code,
    );

    for inactive_col in [
        &input.post_helper.staged.bool_value,
        &input.post_helper.staged.pending_node_tag,
        &input.post_helper.staged.selector,
        &input.post_helper.staged.type_test_ok,
    ] {
        b.gated_eq(enable, inactive_col.clone(), immediate(0));
    }

    // A14: complete named helper preservation (including reg_a and operand_b) + caller rest.
    for (pre, post) in [
        (&input.pre_helper.present, &input.post_helper.present),
        (&input.pre_helper.kind, &input.post_helper.kind),
        (&input.pre_helper.site, &input.post_helper.site),
        (&input.pre_helper.mode, &input.post_helper.mode),
        (
            &input.pre_helper.operand_a.space,
            &input.post_helper.operand_a.space,
        ),
        (
            &input.pre_helper.operand_a.index,
            &input.post_helper.operand_a.index,
        ),
        (
            &input.pre_helper.operand_b.present,
            &input.post_helper.operand_b.present,
        ),
        (
            &input.pre_helper.operand_b.value.space,
            &input.post_helper.operand_b.value.space,
        ),
        (
            &input.pre_helper.operand_b.value.index,
            &input.post_helper.operand_b.value.index,
        ),
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .chain(eff_reg_a.iter().zip(input.post_helper.reg_a.iter()))
    .chain(
        input
            .pre_helper
            .rest
            .iter()
            .zip(input.post_helper.rest.iter()),
    ) {
        b.gated_eq(enable, post.clone(), pre.clone());
    }

    // A15-A17: enable-effective bytewise counter ranges and exact [-1, +1, +1, +1] deltas.
    let pre_remaining = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.pre_counters.remaining_work);
    let pre_consumed = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.pre_counters.consumed_work);
    let pre_helper_work = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.pre_counters.helper_work);
    let pre_visits = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.pre_counters.helper_visits);
    let post_remaining = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.post_counters.remaining_work);
    let post_consumed = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.post_counters.consumed_work);
    let post_helper_work = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.post_counters.helper_work);
    let post_visits = eff_bytes(&mut b, enable, &input.post_counters.helper_visits);

    decrement(&mut b, enable, &pre_remaining, &post_remaining);
    increment(&mut b, enable, &pre_consumed, &post_consumed);
    increment(&mut b, enable, &pre_helper_work, &post_helper_work);
    increment(&mut b, enable, &pre_visits, &post_visits);

    Ok(BScalarArithmeticSubBlock {
        instructions: b.code,
        read_tag,
        read_n,
        b_is_uint,
        underflow,
        borrow_chain,
        preserved: input.clone(),
    })
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum BScalarArithmeticFieldRole {
    Enable,
    ConstActiveRows,
    ConstRowTag { row: usize },
    ConstRowByte { row: usize, byte: usize },
    DataActiveRows,
    DataRowTag { row: usize },
    DataRowByte { row: usize, byte: usize },
    PreHelperLane { index: usize },
    PostHelperLane { index: usize },
    PreCounterByte { counter: usize, byte: usize },
    PostCounterByte { counter: usize, byte: usize },
    ReadTag,
    ReadByte { byte: usize },
    BIsUInt,
    Underflow,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct BScalarArithmeticField {
    pub role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole,
    pub kind: Type,
}

/// Standalone `IrSource` wrapper around `emit_b_scalar_arithmetic_sub` with
/// explicit `PublicInput` / `Impact` / `Output` ordering for inspection and
/// downstream bridge consumption. Makes no general ledger or full-B claim.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct EmittedBScalarArithmeticSub {
    pub ir: IrSource,
    pub input_fields: Vec<BScalarArithmeticField>,
    pub output_fields: Vec<BScalarArithmeticField>,
    pub const_capacity: usize,
    pub data_capacity: usize,
    pub rest_lanes: usize,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct BScalarArithmeticWitness {
    pub enable: bool,
    pub const_active_rows: u128,
    pub const_rows: Vec<(u128, [u8; 16])>,
    pub data_active_rows: u128,
    pub data_rows: Vec<(u128, [u8; 16])>,
    pub pre_helper_lanes: Vec<u128>,
    pub post_helper_lanes: Vec<u128>,
    pub pre_counters: [u128; 4],
    pub post_counters: [u128; 4],
}

impl EmittedBScalarArithmeticSub {
    pub fn pack_witness(
        &self,
        w: &BScalarArithmeticWitness,
    ) -> Result<Vec<Fr>, BScalarArithmeticConstructionError> {
        if w.const_rows.len() != self.const_capacity {
            return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RowCount {
                space: BValueSpace::Const,
            });
        }
        if w.data_rows.len() != self.data_capacity {
            return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RowCount {
                space: BValueSpace::Data,
            });
        }
        let expected_helper_lanes = 48 + self.rest_lanes;
        if w.pre_helper_lanes.len() != expected_helper_lanes
            || w.post_helper_lanes.len() != expected_helper_lanes
        {
            return Err(BScalarArithmeticConstructionError::RestLaneCount);
        }
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(self.input_fields.len());
        out.push(Fr::from(u128::from(w.enable)));
        out.push(Fr::from(w.const_active_rows));
        for (tag, bytes) in &w.const_rows {
            out.push(Fr::from(*tag));
            out.extend(bytes.iter().map(|b| Fr::from(u128::from(*b))));
        }
        out.push(Fr::from(w.data_active_rows));
        for (tag, bytes) in &w.data_rows {
            out.push(Fr::from(*tag));
            out.extend(bytes.iter().map(|b| Fr::from(u128::from(*b))));
        }
        out.extend(w.pre_helper_lanes.iter().copied().map(Fr::from));
        out.extend(w.post_helper_lanes.iter().copied().map(Fr::from));
        for c in w.pre_counters {
            out.extend(c.to_le_bytes().into_iter().map(|b| Fr::from(u128::from(b))));
        }
        for c in w.post_counters {
            out.extend(c.to_le_bytes().into_iter().map(|b| Fr::from(u128::from(b))));
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}

pub fn emit_public_b_scalar_arithmetic_sub(
    const_capacity: usize,
    data_capacity: usize,
    rest_lanes: usize,
) -> Result<EmittedBScalarArithmeticSub, BScalarArithmeticConstructionError> {
    let mut input_fields = vec![
        BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::Enable,
            kind: Type::Bool,
        },
        BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::ConstActiveRows,
            kind: Type::UInt,
        },
    ];
    for row in 0..const_capacity {
        input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::ConstRowTag { row },
            kind: Type::UInt,
        });
        for byte in 0..16 {
            input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
                role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::ConstRowByte { row, byte },
                kind: Type::UInt,
            });
        }
    }
    input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
        role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::DataActiveRows,
        kind: Type::UInt,
    });
    for row in 0..data_capacity {
        input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::DataRowTag { row },
            kind: Type::UInt,
        });
        for byte in 0..16 {
            input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
                role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::DataRowByte { row, byte },
                kind: Type::UInt,
            });
        }
    }
    let helper_lane_count = 48 + rest_lanes;
    for index in 0..helper_lane_count {
        input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::PreHelperLane { index },
            kind: Type::UInt,
        });
    }
    for index in 0..helper_lane_count {
        input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::PostHelperLane { index },
            kind: Type::UInt,
        });
    }
    for counter in 0..4 {
        for byte in 0..16 {
            input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
                role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::PreCounterByte { counter, byte },
                kind: Type::UInt,
            });
        }
    }
    for counter in 0..4 {
        for byte in 0..16 {
            input_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
                role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::PostCounterByte { counter, byte },
                kind: Type::UInt,
            });
        }
    }

    let mut instructions = Vec::new();
    let mut ops = Vec::with_capacity(input_fields.len());
    for (position, field) in input_fields.iter().enumerate() {
        let id = Identifier(format!("%b_scalar_sub.input.{position}"));
        instructions.push(Instruction::PublicInput {
            guard: None,
            val_t: IrType::Native,
            output: id.clone(),
        });
        let value = Operand::Variable(id);
        constrain(&mut instructions, value.clone(), field.kind);
        instructions.push(Instruction::Impact {
            guard: immediate(1),
            inputs: vec![value.clone()],
        });
        ops.push(value);
    }

    let mut cursor = 0usize;
    let enable = ops[cursor].clone();
    cursor += 1;
    let const_active_rows = ops[cursor].clone();
    cursor += 1;
    let mut const_rows = Vec::with_capacity(const_capacity);
    for _ in 0..const_capacity {
        let tag = ops[cursor].clone();
        cursor += 1;
        let n = std::array::from_fn(|i| ops[cursor + i].clone());
        cursor += 16;
        const_rows.push(BValueRowLanes { tag, n });
    }
    let data_active_rows = ops[cursor].clone();
    cursor += 1;
    let mut data_rows = Vec::with_capacity(data_capacity);
    for _ in 0..data_capacity {
        let tag = ops[cursor].clone();
        cursor += 1;
        let n = std::array::from_fn(|i| ops[cursor + i].clone());
        cursor += 16;
        data_rows.push(BValueRowLanes { tag, n });
    }
    let pre_helper = unpack_helper_lanes(&ops[cursor..cursor + helper_lane_count]);
    cursor += helper_lane_count;
    let post_helper = unpack_helper_lanes(&ops[cursor..cursor + helper_lane_count]);
    cursor += helper_lane_count;
    let pre_counters = unpack_counter_lanes(&ops[cursor..cursor + 64]);
    cursor += 64;
    let post_counters = unpack_counter_lanes(&ops[cursor..cursor + 64]);

    let inputs = BScalarArithmeticSubInputs {
        enable,
        constants: BValueTableRead {
            capacity: const_capacity,
            active_rows: const_active_rows,
            rows: const_rows,
        },
        data: BValueTableRead {
            capacity: data_capacity,
            active_rows: data_active_rows,
            rows: data_rows,
        },
        pre_helper,
        post_helper,
        pre_counters,
        post_counters,
    };
    let block = emit_b_scalar_arithmetic_sub(&inputs, "b_scalar_sub")?;
    instructions.extend(block.instructions);

    let mut outputs = Vec::with_capacity(19 + ops.len());
    outputs.push(block.read_tag);
    outputs.extend(block.read_n);
    outputs.push(block.b_is_uint);
    outputs.push(block.underflow);
    outputs.extend(ops);

    let mut output_fields = vec![BScalarArithmeticField {
        role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::ReadTag,
        kind: Type::UInt,
    }];
    for byte in 0..16 {
        output_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
            role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::ReadByte { byte },
            kind: Type::UInt,
        });
    }
    output_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
        role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::BIsUInt,
        kind: Type::Bool,
    });
    output_fields.push(BScalarArithmeticField {
        role: BScalarArithmeticFieldRole::Underflow,
        kind: Type::Bool,
    });
    output_fields.extend(input_fields.iter().cloned());

    instructions.push(Instruction::Output { vals: outputs });
    let ir = IrSource {
        version: Default::default(),
        inputs: vec![],
        outputs: vec![IrType::Native; output_fields.len()],
        do_communications_commitment: false,
        instructions: Arc::new(instructions),
    };
    Ok(EmittedBScalarArithmeticSub {
        ir,
        input_fields,
        output_fields,
        const_capacity,
        data_capacity,
        rest_lanes,
    })
}

fn unpack_helper_lanes(slice: &[Operand]) -> BScalarArithmeticRegister {
    BScalarArithmeticRegister {
        present: slice[0].clone(),
        kind: slice[1].clone(),
        site: slice[2].clone(),
        mode: slice[3].clone(),
        phase: slice[4].clone(),
        operand_a: BValueRef {
            space: slice[5].clone(),
            index: slice[6].clone(),
        },
        operand_b: BOptValueRef {
            present: slice[7].clone(),
            value: BValueRef {
                space: slice[8].clone(),
                index: slice[9].clone(),
            },
        },
        reg_a: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[10 + i].clone()),
        staged: BScalarStagedLanes {
            tag: slice[26].clone(),
            u128_value: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[27 + i].clone()),
            bool_value: slice[43].clone(),
            pending_node_tag: slice[44].clone(),
            prim_error_code: slice[45].clone(),
            selector: slice[46].clone(),
            type_test_ok: slice[47].clone(),
        },
        rest: slice[48..].to_vec(),
    }
}

fn unpack_counter_lanes(slice: &[Operand]) -> BChargedCounters {
    BChargedCounters {
        remaining_work: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[i].clone()),
        consumed_work: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[16 + i].clone()),
        helper_work: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[32 + i].clone()),
        helper_visits: std::array::from_fn(|i| slice[48 + i].clone()),
    }
}

fn table_operands(table: &BValueTableRead) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Operand> {
    table
        .rows
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|row| std::iter::once(&row.tag).chain(row.n.iter()))
}

fn helper_operands(helper: &BScalarArithmeticRegister) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Operand> {
    [
        &helper.present,
        &helper.kind,
        &helper.site,
        &helper.mode,
        &helper.phase,
        &helper.operand_a.space,
        &helper.operand_a.index,
        &helper.operand_b.present,
        &helper.operand_b.value.space,
        &helper.operand_b.value.index,
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .chain(helper.reg_a.iter())
    .chain(std::iter::once(&helper.staged.tag))
    .chain(helper.staged.u128_value.iter())
    .chain([
        &helper.staged.bool_value,
        &helper.staged.pending_node_tag,
        &helper.staged.prim_error_code,
        &helper.staged.selector,
        &helper.staged.type_test_ok,
    ])
    .chain(helper.rest.iter())
}

fn counter_operands(counters: &BChargedCounters) -> impl Iterator<Item = &Operand> {
    [
        &counters.remaining_work,
        &counters.consumed_work,
        &counters.helper_work,
        &counters.helper_visits,
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .flatten()
}

struct Emitter {
    scope: String,
    serial: usize,
    code: Vec<Instruction>,
}

impl Emitter {
    fn fresh(&mut self) -> Identifier {
        let id = Identifier(format!("%{}.local.temp.{}", self.scope, self.serial));
        self.serial += 1;
        id
    }

    fn select(&mut self, bit: Operand, a: Operand, b: Operand) -> Operand {
        let output = self.fresh();
        self.code.push(Instruction::CondSelect {
            bit,
            a,
            b,
            output: output.clone(),
        });
        Operand::Variable(output)
    }

    fn eq(&mut self, a: Operand, b: Operand) {
        self.code.push(Instruction::ConstrainEq { a, b });
    }

    fn gated_eq(&mut self, enable: &Operand, a: Operand, b: Operand) {
        let guarded = self.select(enable.clone(), a, b.clone());
        self.eq(guarded, b);
    }

    fn boolean(&mut self, val: Operand) {
        self.code.push(Instruction::ConstrainToBoolean { val });
    }

    fn bits(&mut self, val: Operand, bits: u32) {
        self.code.push(Instruction::ConstrainBits { val, bits });
    }

    fn assert(&mut self, cond: Operand) {
        self.code.push(Instruction::Assert { cond });
    }

    fn test_eq(&mut self, a: Operand, other: Operand) -> Operand {
        let output = self.fresh();
        self.code.push(Instruction::TestEq {
            a,
            b: other,
            output: output.clone(),
        });
        let value = Operand::Variable(output);
        self.boolean(value.clone());
        value
    }

    fn less_than(&mut self, a: Operand, b: Operand) -> Operand {
        let output = self.fresh();
        self.code.push(Instruction::LessThan {
            a,
            b,
            bits: 128,
            output: output.clone(),
        });
        let value = Operand::Variable(output);
        self.boolean(value.clone());
        value
    }

    fn add(&mut self, a: Operand, b: Operand) -> Operand {
        let output = self.fresh();
        self.code.push(Instruction::Add {
            a,
            b,
            output: output.clone(),
        });
        Operand::Variable(output)
    }

    fn mul(&mut self, a: Operand, b: Operand) -> Operand {
        let output = self.fresh();
        self.code.push(Instruction::Mul {
            a,
            b,
            output: output.clone(),
        });
        Operand::Variable(output)
    }
}

fn space_read_any_kind(
    b: &mut Emitter,
    table: &BValueTableRead,
    enable: Operand,
    index: Operand,
    scope: String,
) -> Result<(Operand, BU128Lanes), BScalarArithmeticConstructionError> {
    if table.capacity == 0 {
        b.eq(enable, immediate(0));
        return Ok((immediate(0), std::array::from_fn(|_| immediate(0))));
    }
    let mut columns = vec![Column {
        name: "tag".into(),
        kind: Type::UInt,
    }];
    columns.extend((0..16).map(|i| Column {
        name: format!("n.{i}"),
        kind: Type::UInt,
    }));
    let shape = TableShape::new(table.capacity, columns)?;
    let rows = table
        .rows
        .iter()
        .map(|row| {
            std::iter::once(row.tag.clone())
                .chain(row.n.iter().cloned())
                .collect()
        })
        .collect();
    let fetch = emit_enabled_fetch_block(
        &shape,
        &EnabledFetchInputs {
            enable,
            active_rows: table.active_rows.clone(),
            index,
            rows,
        },
        &scope,
    )?;
    b.code.extend(fetch.instructions);
    Ok((
        fetch.selected[0].value.clone(),
        std::array::from_fn(|i| fetch.selected[i + 1].value.clone()),
    ))
}

fn eff_bytes(b: &mut Emitter, enable: &Operand, lanes: &BU128Lanes) -> BU128Lanes {
    std::array::from_fn(|i| {
        let value = b.select(enable.clone(), lanes[i].clone(), immediate(0));
        b.bits(value.clone(), 8);
        value
    })
}

fn decrement(b: &mut Emitter, enable: &Operand, pre: &BU128Lanes, post: &BU128Lanes) {
    let mut borrow = enable.clone();
    for i in 0..16 {
        let zero = b.test_eq(pre[i].clone(), immediate(0));
        let next = b.select(borrow.clone(), zero, immediate(0));
        b.boolean(next.clone());
        let carry = b.mul(immediate(256), next.clone());
        b.bits(carry.clone(), 9);
        let left = b.add(pre[i].clone(), carry);
        b.bits(left.clone(), 9);
        let right = b.add(post[i].clone(), borrow);
        b.bits(right.clone(), 9);
        b.eq(left, right);
        borrow = next;
    }
    b.eq(borrow, immediate(0));
}

fn increment(b: &mut Emitter, enable: &Operand, pre: &BU128Lanes, post: &BU128Lanes) {
    let mut carry = enable.clone();
    for i in 0..16 {
        let full = b.test_eq(pre[i].clone(), immediate(255));
        let next = b.select(carry.clone(), full, immediate(0));
        b.boolean(next.clone());
        let overflow = b.mul(immediate(256), next.clone());
        b.bits(overflow.clone(), 9);
        let left = b.add(post[i].clone(), overflow);
        b.bits(left.clone(), 9);
        let right = b.add(pre[i].clone(), carry);
        b.bits(right.clone(), 9);
        b.eq(left, right);
        carry = next;
    }
    b.eq(carry, immediate(0));
}

#[path = "b_scalar_subtraction_three_tests.rs"]
mod three_tests;
```
