## W3-13 FULL FILE crates/mori-backend/src/sherlock_machine/mod.rs

```rust
//! Source-only bounded scalar fetch, composable A102 Literal instructions, and
//! trusted-source A102 witness construction.
//!
//! The `fetch`, `enabled_fetch`, and `literal_step` constructors emit pinned SDK
//! instructions over caller-supplied table shapes and operands without loading,
//! evaluating, compiling, or proving them. The child module `literal_bridge`
//! adds statement binding and local native and ledger entry points around the
//! unmodified `literal_step` fragment; it is uncompiled and unobserved. The child
//! module `literal_witness` is a host diagnostic producer (H1, zero native
//! authority) that invokes `mori_core::sherlock::layout_a::prepare_a`,
//! `mori_core::sherlock::Machine::new(...).step()` for the first A102 `StepRecord`,
//! `encode_observed_a` for the canonical pre-state, and an isolated Step-mode
//! Constant-space post-state constructor without calling `decode_observed_a` on
//! constructed Step-mode rows. Full `StepInv` (`O1`..`O4`), `uiValid`, `F4`,
//! `OBL.G4` canonical byte binding, `projectA`, and exactly-one dispatch remain
//! open caller obligations.

use midnight_transient_crypto::curve::Fr;
use midnight_zkir_v3::ir::Operand;
use midnight_zkir_v3::ir_types::IrType;
use midnight_zkir_v3::{Identifier, Instruction, IrSource};
use mori_core::{Type, Value};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use std::sync::Arc;

mod fetch;
pub use fetch::emit_fetch_block;

mod enabled_fetch;
pub use enabled_fetch::{
    emit_enabled_fetch_block, EnabledFetchBlock, EnabledFetchInputs, EnabledTypedOperand,
};

pub mod literal_host;
mod literal_step;
mod literal_ui_valid;
pub mod literal_bridge;
pub mod literal_witness;
pub mod return_publication_step;
pub mod return_publication_witness;
pub mod literal_complete_execution;
pub mod literal_complete_bridge;
pub mod error_publication_step;
pub mod fuel_exhaustion_step;
pub use literal_step::{
    emit_literal_step, literal_schema, LiteralCapacities, LiteralConstructionError,
    LiteralEnvelope, LiteralImage, LiteralLane, LiteralSchema, LiteralStepBlock,
    LiteralStepInputs, LiteralTable,
};
pub use literal_ui_valid::{
    emit_literal_ui_valid, emit_literal_ui_valid_with_capacities, is_reserved_ui_valid_variable,
    schema_capacities, LiteralUiValidBlock, LiteralUiValidInputs,
};
pub use literal_witness::{
    build_checked_literal_transition_request, build_literal_transition_request,
    core_program_capacities, core_state_capacities, encode_a102_step_post_state,
    produce_a102_literal_transition, produce_a102_literal_transition_from_step,
    program_block_to_image_values, state_block_to_image_values, validate_literal_schema,
    ProducedLiteralTransition,
};
pub use fuel_exhaustion_step::{
    emit_fuel_exhaustion_step, is_reserved_fuel_exhaustion_variable, FuelExhaustionStepBlock,
    FuelExhaustionStepInputs, FUEL_EXHAUSTION_RULE_CHARGE, FUEL_EXHAUSTION_RULE_ID,
    FUEL_EXHAUSTION_RULE_START_LINE, FUEL_EXHAUSTION_RULE_TAG,
};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Column {
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: Type,
}

/// Host construction shape, not a machine encoding or resource admission.
/// No fixed maximum is chosen here. A later owner must admit concrete bounds.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TableShape {
    capacity: usize,
    columns: Vec<Column>,
    cells: usize,
}

pub use literal_host::ConstructionError;

impl TableShape {
    pub fn new(capacity: usize, columns: Vec<Column>) -> Result<Self, ConstructionError> {
        if capacity == 0 || columns.is_empty() {
            return Err(ConstructionError::EmptyShape);
        }
        let mut names = BTreeSet::new();
        for column in &columns {
            if column.name.is_empty() || !names.insert(column.name.clone()) {
                return Err(ConstructionError::EmptyOrRepeatedColumnName);
            }
        }
        let cells = capacity.checked_mul(columns.len()).ok_or(ConstructionError::SizeOverflow)?;
        cells.checked_add(columns.len()).and_then(|n| n.checked_add(2))
            .ok_or(ConstructionError::SizeOverflow)?;
        u128::try_from(capacity).map_err(|_| ConstructionError::SizeOverflow)?;
        Ok(Self { capacity, columns, cells })
    }

    pub fn capacity(&self) -> usize { self.capacity }
    pub fn columns(&self) -> &[Column] { &self.columns }

    fn check_rows<T>(&self, rows: &[Vec<T>]) -> Result<(), ConstructionError> {
        if rows.len() != self.capacity { return Err(ConstructionError::RowCount); }
        for (row, cells) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
            if cells.len() != self.columns.len() {
                return Err(ConstructionError::ColumnCount { row });
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Caller-owned Native operands. The block constrains count/index as UInt128
/// and every cell according to TableShape; all rows, including inactive rows,
/// are supplied and preserved. No canonical padding convention is imposed.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct FetchInputs {
    pub active_rows: Operand,
    pub index: Operand,
    pub rows: Vec<Vec<Operand>>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct TypedOperand {
    pub kind: Type,
    pub value: Operand,
}

/// An instruction fragment, without public bindings or an Output terminator.
/// Preserved operands are exact aliases, not separately supplied after-state.
pub struct FetchBlock {
    pub instructions: Vec<Instruction>,
    pub selected: Vec<TypedOperand>,
    pub preserved: FetchInputs,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum FieldRole {
    ActiveRows,
    Index,
    Cell { row: usize, column: usize },
    Selected { column: usize },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Field {
    pub role: FieldRole,
    pub kind: Type,
}

/// Standalone construction for review and future normal comparison. No SDK
/// execution occurs. Inputs follow PublicInput encounter order; outputs follow
/// the Output operand list. See MAPPING.md for the complete positional map.
pub struct EmittedFetch {
    pub ir: IrSource,
    pub input_fields: Vec<Field>,
    pub output_fields: Vec<Field>,
    shape: TableShape,
}

pub struct FetchWitness {
    pub active_rows: u128,
    pub index: u128,
    pub rows: Vec<Vec<Value>>,
}

impl EmittedFetch {
    /// Encode supplied scalars in public-input order. Only host shape/type is
    /// checked. In particular, this does not select a row, compute an expected
    /// result, or decide index membership/constraint acceptance.
    pub fn pack_witness(&self, witness: &FetchWitness) -> Result<Vec<Fr>, ConstructionError> {
        self.shape.check_rows(&witness.rows)?;
        let mut packed = Vec::with_capacity(self.shape.cells + 2);
        packed.push(Fr::from(witness.active_rows));
        packed.push(Fr::from(witness.index));
        for (row, cells) in witness.rows.iter().enumerate() {
            for (column, value) in cells.iter().enumerate() {
                if value.ty() != self.shape.columns[column].kind {
                    return Err(ConstructionError::CellType { row, column });
                }
                packed.push(Fr::from(match value {
                    Value::UInt(n) => n.0,
                    Value::Bool(b) => u128::from(*b),
                }));
            }
        }
        Ok(packed)
    }
}

fn immediate(n: u128) -> Operand { Operand::Immediate(Fr::from(n)) }

fn constrain(instructions: &mut Vec<Instruction>, value: Operand, kind: Type) {
    instructions.push(match kind {
        Type::UInt => Instruction::ConstrainBits { val: value, bits: 128 },
        Type::Bool => Instruction::ConstrainToBoolean { val: value },
    });
}

/// Construct a public-input wrapper using the existing backend's
/// PublicInput/range/Impact convention. Binding/security review is separate.
pub fn emit_public_fetch(shape: &TableShape) -> Result<EmittedFetch, ConstructionError> {
    let mut instructions = Vec::new();
    let mut input_fields = vec![
        Field { role: FieldRole::ActiveRows, kind: Type::UInt },
        Field { role: FieldRole::Index, kind: Type::UInt },
    ];
    for row in 0..shape.capacity {
        for (column, col) in shape.columns.iter().enumerate() {
            input_fields.push(Field { role: FieldRole::Cell { row, column }, kind: col.kind });
        }
    }
    let mut operands = Vec::with_capacity(input_fields.len());
    for (position, field) in input_fields.iter().enumerate() {
        let id = Identifier(format!("%row_fetch.input.{position}"));
        instructions.push(Instruction::PublicInput {
            guard: None, val_t: IrType::Native, output: id.clone(),
        });
        let value = Operand::Variable(id);
        constrain(&mut instructions, value.clone(), field.kind);
        instructions.push(Instruction::Impact {
            guard: immediate(1), inputs: vec![value.clone()],
        });
        operands.push(value);
    }
    let inputs = FetchInputs {
        active_rows: operands[0].clone(),
        index: operands[1].clone(),
        rows: operands[2..].chunks(shape.columns.len()).map(|row| row.to_vec()).collect(),
    };
    let block = emit_fetch_block(shape, &inputs, "row_fetch")?;
    instructions.extend(block.instructions);
    let mut outputs: Vec<_> = block.selected.into_iter().map(|cell| cell.value).collect();
    outputs.push(block.preserved.active_rows);
    outputs.push(block.preserved.index);
    outputs.extend(block.preserved.rows.into_iter().flatten());
    let mut output_fields: Vec<_> = shape.columns.iter().enumerate().map(|(column, col)| {
        Field { role: FieldRole::Selected { column }, kind: col.kind }
    }).collect();
    output_fields.extend(input_fields.iter().cloned());
    instructions.push(Instruction::Output { vals: outputs });
    let ir = IrSource {
        version: Default::default(),
        inputs: vec![],
        outputs: vec![IrType::Native; output_fields.len()],
        do_communications_commitment: false,
        instructions: Arc::new(instructions),
    };
    Ok(EmittedFetch { ir, input_fields, output_fields, shape: shape.clone() })
}
```

## PUBLIC SDK: complete IrSource.load method only (not preprocess/prove)
Source public SDK commit04c9c5d9bcebb8d4427d8589fb54d58a55599c14, zkir-v3/src/ir.rs exact rawlines954..989.

```rust
    /// Attempts to parse an arbitrary input as IR.
    pub fn load<R: Read>(reader: R) -> io::Result<Self> {
        let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_reader(reader)?;
        match value {
            serde_json::Value::Object(mut obj) => {
                let ver = serde_json::from_value(
                    obj.get("version")
                        .ok_or(io::Error::new(
                            io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                            "Expected a version entry",
                        ))?
                        .clone(),
                )?;
                match ver {
                    SerdeVersion {
                        major: 3,
                        minor: 0..=0,
                    } => {
                        obj.insert(
                            "version".into(),
                            serde_json::Value::Number(ver.minor.into()),
                        );
                        Ok(serde_json::from_value(serde_json::Value::Object(obj))?)
                    }
                    SerdeVersion { major, minor } => Err(io::Error::new(
                        io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                        format!("Unhandled version: {major}.{minor}"),
                    )),
                }
            }
            _ => Err(io::Error::new(
                io::ErrorKind::InvalidData,
                "Expected a JSON object",
            )),
        }
    }
```

```python
"""One-off Root-selected ordinary R435 executor. No imports from product code.
Uses the already reviewed normal subprocess/timeout/prlimit/capture class.
No work is staged or run before an explicit Root decision and exact pin checks.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse, datetime, hashlib, json, os, re, selectors, shlex, shutil, signal, subprocess, time

D = Path('/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-ordinary-QA')
E = Path('/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-execution-preparation')
CAPTURE = E / 'actual-thirteen-01'
# Filled once during source preparation, not computed from mutable selection.
FROZEN = {'proposal': {'path': '/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-ordinary-QA/PROPOSED.json', 'bytes': 29898, 'sha256': '4ad42defa549cef2f5c3a6ac8d688457a8759d3634af8fefd779b1b9dad6d620'}, 'DAG': {'path': '/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-ordinary-QA/READSET-AND-OUTPUT-DAG.json', 'bytes': 13177, 'sha256': 'f153e2874e422a9195b4d297a5f6053644c4c491bc80c5c55551f6f3dcd3bf97'}, 'flat_inputs': {'path': '/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-ordinary-QA/FLAT-INPUT-PINS.json', 'bytes': 135220, 'sha256': 'cd49f9e88c82155f46fa0935a53f84fa2069dddb29d6e7060cfcf08379c1b48f'}, 'composition': {'path': '/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/435-resolved-signature-ordinary-QA/SOURCE-COMPOSITION.json', 'bytes': 167929, 'sha256': 'a95f7361b4fa4d13349fa2fa1541e24089d65f650c65bf72bdbfae5d44136ea6'}}

REVIEW_REPORT = {'path': '/home/charl/Moriarty/.moriarty-dev/runtime/sherlock-review-20261017/433-generic-resolved-signature-combined-review-preparation/final-review-01/REPORT.md', 'bytes': 31481, 'sha256': '5e4717cdf0677e272b76a03a09671f096ec0eae5467919b16c879af325a498ae'}

def pin(path):
    path = Path(path)
    raw = path.read_bytes()
    return dict(path=str(path), bytes=len(raw), sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest())


def check(row):
    actual = pin(row['path'])
    assert actual['bytes'] == row['bytes'] and actual['sha256'] == row['sha256'], row['path']
    return actual


def save(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    data = (json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n').encode()
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + '.partial')
    with temporary.open('xb') as stream:
        stream.write(data)
    os.replace(temporary, path)


def static_check(rows):
    cache = {}
    results = []
    for row in rows:
        path = Path(row['path'])
        real = str(path.resolve(strict=True))
        if real not in cache:
            cache[real] = pin(path)
        actual = cache[real]
        results.append(dict(path=str(path), match=actual['bytes'] == row['bytes'] and actual['sha256'] == row['sha256']))
    return dict(files=results, all_match=all(row['match'] for row in results))


def stop_group(process):
    try:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        pass


def capture(op, phase):
    """Same finite ordinary pattern: exact argv/env, binary bounded pipes, one child."""
    save(phase / 'COMMAND.json', op)
    began = time.monotonic()
    deadline = began + op['wallSeconds']
    streams = {}
    process = None
    failure = None
    capture_complete = False
    poller = selectors.DefaultSelector()
    try:
        process = subprocess.Popen(op['argv'], cwd=op['cwd'], env=op['environment'], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
        for name, pipe in [('stdout', process.stdout), ('stderr', process.stderr)]:
            out = (phase / (name + '.bin')).open('xb')
            streams[name] = dict(pipe=pipe, out=out, seen=0, retained=0, path=phase / (name + '.bin'))
            poller.register(pipe, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
        while poller.get_map():
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining <= 0 and failure is None:
                failure = 'wall deadline'
                stop_group(process)
                break
            for key, _ in poller.select(timeout=min(0.1, max(0.0, remaining)) if failure is None else 0.1):
                record = streams[key.data]
                block = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 65536)
                if not block:
                    poller.unregister(key.fileobj)
                    key.fileobj.close()
                    continue
                record['seen'] += len(block)
                keep = block[:max(0, op['keptBytesPerStream'] - record['retained'])]
                record['out'].write(keep)
                record['retained'] += len(keep)
                if record['seen'] > op['keptBytesPerStream'] and failure is None:
                    failure = key.data + ' capture cap exceeded'
                    stop_group(process)
            if failure is not None:
                break
        capture_complete = not poller.get_map()
        try:
            code = process.wait(timeout=max(0.001, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            failure = failure or 'wall deadline'
            stop_group(process)
            code = process.poll()
    except BaseException as error:
        failure = failure or type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error)
        if process is not None:
            stop_group(process)
            try:
                process.wait(timeout=max(0.001, deadline - time.monotonic()))
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                pass
        code = process.returncode if process is not None else None
    finally:
        poller.close()
        for record in streams.values():
            record['out'].close()
            record['pipe'].close()
    return dict(id=op['id'], actual_exit=code, elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic() - began,
                controller_failure=failure, streams=[dict(**pin(r['path']), bytes_seen=r['seen'], bytes_retained=r['retained'],
                full_stream_retained=capture_complete and r['seen'] == r['retained']) for r in streams.values()])


def dep_inputs(path, allowed):
    text = Path(path).read_text().replace('\\\n', ' ')
    read = set()
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith('#'):
            assert not line.startswith('# env-dep:'), 'undeclared env-dep in dep-info'
            continue
        _, separator, rest = line.partition(': ')
        if separator:
            read.update(shlex.split(rest, comments=False, posix=True))
    assert read, 'empty dep-info read set'
    observed = []
    for item in sorted(read):
        path = Path(item)
        assert path.is_absolute(), 'unexpected relative dep-info input: ' + item
        real = str(path.resolve(strict=True))
        assert real in allowed, 'unknown dep-info input: ' + item
        row = allowed[real]
        actual = pin(path)
        assert actual['bytes'] == row['bytes'] and actual['sha256'] == row['sha256'], 'changed dep-info input: ' + item
        observed.append(dict(**actual, selected_binding=row['path']))
    return observed


def test_membership(op, phase):
    expected = op['expected']['exactTest']
    text = (phase / 'stdout.bin').read_text()
    results = re.findall(r'(?m)^test (\S+) \.\.\. (\S+)$', text)
    assert re.search(r'(?m)^running 1 test\s*$', text), 'missing exact one-test announcement'
    assert results == [(expected, 'ok')], 'test selector/result mismatch or zero tests'
    summary = re.search(r'(?m)^test result: ok\. (\d+) passed; (\d+) failed; (\d+) ignored; (\d+) measured; (\d+) filtered out;', text)
    assert summary and tuple(map(int, summary.groups()[:4])) == (1, 0, 0, 0), 'not exactly one passing selected test'
    return dict(exactTest=expected, selected=1, passed=1, failed=0, ignored=0, measured=0,
                filtered_out=int(summary.group(5)))


def main(selection_path):
    selection = json.loads(Path(selection_path).read_text())
    assert selection['authorized_one_attempt'] is True
    decision = selection['Root_review_decision']
    assert decision['Root_read_full_report'] is True
    assert decision['source_accepted'] is True and decision['all_13_operations_eligible'] is True
    assert decision['actual_full_page_coverage'] == 87
    assert decision['report'] == REVIEW_REPORT
    check(REVIEW_REPORT)
    assert selection['frozen'] == FROZEN
    for row in FROZEN.values():
        check(row)
    check(selection['helper'])
    assert selection['helper'] == pin(Path(__file__))
    assert selection['capture_directory'] == str(CAPTURE)
    assert not CAPTURE.exists() and not (E / 'ONE-ATTEMPT.json').exists()
    proposal = json.loads(Path(FROZEN['proposal']['path']).read_text())
    flat = json.loads(Path(FROZEN['flat_inputs']['path']).read_text())['files']
    composition = json.loads(Path(FROZEN['composition']['path']).read_text())
    dag = json.loads(Path(FROZEN['DAG']['path']).read_text())
    ops = proposal['operations']
    assert len(ops) == 13 and len(flat) == 484
    assert selection['selected_operation_ids'] == [op['id'] for op in ops]
    assert all(op['keptBytesPerStream'] == 131072 for op in ops)
    for directory in [D / 'build', D / 'tmp']:
        assert not directory.is_symlink()
        assert not directory.exists() or (directory.is_dir() and not any(directory.iterdir())), 'output workdir is not fresh/empty'
    assert all(not Path(output).exists() for op in ops for output in op.get('outputs', []))
    # Only after explicit Root selection: reconstruct missing reviewed inputs, never overwrite existing immutable inputs.
    snapshots = composition['files']
    expected_paths = {row['snapshot']['path'] for row in snapshots}
    if (D / 'snapshot').exists():
        assert {str(p) for p in (D / 'snapshot').rglob('*') if p.is_file()} == expected_paths
    claim = dict(selection=pin(selection_path), helper=pin(__file__), started_utc=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                 mode='one exact ordinary attempt; no retry', argv_policy='unchanged frozen proposal')
    with (E / 'ONE-ATTEMPT.json').open('x') as stream:
        json.dump(claim, stream, indent=2)
        stream.write('\n')
    CAPTURE.mkdir(mode=0o700)
    save(CAPTURE / 'ROOT-SELECTION.json', selection)
    try:
        staged = []
        for row in snapshots:
            target = Path(row['snapshot']['path'])
            if target.exists():
                check(row['snapshot'])
                action = 'reused exact immutable reviewed snapshot'
            else:
                check(row['selected'])
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                with target.open('xb') as out, Path(row['selected']['path']).open('rb') as src:
                    shutil.copyfileobj(src, out)
                check(row['snapshot'])
                action = 'materialized exact frozen selected source after selection'
            staged.append(dict(path=str(target), action=action))
        assert {str(p) for p in (D / 'snapshot').rglob('*') if p.is_file()} == expected_paths
        save(CAPTURE / 'STAGING.json', dict(files=staged, snapshot_files=len(staged), Core_files=157, candidate_overlays=9, consumer_data_files=4))
        for directory in [D / 'build', D / 'tmp']:
            directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        pre = static_check(flat)
        save(CAPTURE / 'PRE-PIN-CHECK.json', pre)
        assert pre['all_match']
        generated = {}
        phases = []
        dag_by_id = {row['id']: row for row in dag['phases']}
        for number, op in enumerate(ops, 1):
            phase = CAPTURE / f'{number:02}'
            phase.mkdir()
            before = static_check(flat)
            save(phase / 'PRE-PIN-CHECK.json', before)
            assert before['all_match']
            for row in generated.values():
                check(row)
            for row in dag_by_id[op['id']]['generated_inputs']:
                assert row['path'] in generated, 'prior generated input not observed'
                check(generated[row['path']])
            assert all(not Path(path).exists() for path in op.get('outputs', []))
            result = capture(op, phase)
            try:
                assert result['actual_exit'] == 0 and result['controller_failure'] is None
                assert all(row['full_stream_retained'] for row in result['streams'])
                outputs = [pin(path) for path in op.get('outputs', [])]
                result['outputs'] = outputs
                allowed = {str(Path(row['path']).resolve(strict=True)): row for row in flat}
                allowed.update({str(Path(row['path']).resolve(strict=True)): row for row in generated.values()})
                info = [path for path in op.get('outputs', []) if path.endswith('.d')]
                result['dep_info_inputs'] = [dict(path=path, inputs=dep_inputs(path, allowed)) for path in info]
                if '--exact' in op['argv']:
                    result['exact_test_acceptance'] = test_membership(op, phase)
                for row in outputs:
                    generated[row['path']] = row
                result['accepted'] = True
            except BaseException as error:
                result['accepted'] = False
                result['acceptance_failure'] = type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error)
            after = static_check(flat)
            save(phase / 'POST-PIN-CHECK.json', after)
            if not after['all_match']:
                result['accepted'] = False
                result['acceptance_failure'] = 'post-operation static input pin mismatch'
            save(phase / 'RESULT.json', result)
            phases.append(result)
            save(CAPTURE / 'RESULT.json', dict(phases=phases, completed_operations=len(phases), selected_operations=13,
                 accepted_new_tests=sum('exact_test_acceptance' in r and r.get('accepted', False) for r in phases),
                 all_13_passed=False, final_pin_check_pending=True, generated=list(generated.values()), retry=False))
            if not result['accepted']:
                raise RuntimeError('fail-stop at ' + op['id'])
        post = static_check(flat)
        save(CAPTURE / 'POST-PIN-CHECK.json', post)
        assert post['all_match']
        assert len(phases) == 13 and sum('exact_test_acceptance' in r for r in phases) == 10
        save(CAPTURE / 'RESULT.json', dict(phases=phases, completed_operations=13, selected_operations=13,
             accepted_new_tests=10, all_13_passed=True, final_pin_check_pending=False,
             static_inputs_unchanged=True, generated=list(generated.values()), retry=False))
    except BaseException as error:
        try:
            save(CAPTURE / 'POST-PIN-CHECK.json', static_check(flat))
        except BaseException as pin_error:
            save(CAPTURE / 'POST-PIN-CHECK-ERROR.json', dict(error=str(pin_error)))
        save(CAPTURE / 'FAILURE.json', dict(error=type(error).__name__ + ': ' + str(error), retry=False, preserve_all_outputs=True))
        raise


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--selection', required=True)
    main(parser.parse_args().selection)
```
